CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告




                                      Liu Yongliang
                                             劉永亮
                                           Chairman
                                                主席




Dear Shareholders,                                                     各位股東：

On behalf of the Board, I hereby present the annual report of the      本人謹代表董事會呈列本集團截至2020年12月
Group for the year ended 31 December 2020.                             31日止年度的年度報告。

Overview                                                               概覽
2020 was a challenging year for the Company. At the beginning of       2020年 對 本 公 司 而 言 是 充 滿 挑 戰 的 一 年。
the year, the sudden outbreak of COVID began to spread across          COVID於年初突然爆發並蔓延全國，公司管理
the country. Our management team maintained a high level of            團隊保持高度警覺，並根據地方各級政府的要
vigilance and actively implemented various prevention and control      求，積極落實各項防控安排和措施。本公司遵
arrangements and measures in accordance with the requirements          從習近平的「中國特色社會主義思想」，並深入
of local governments at all levels. We focus on the guidance of        貫徹落實管理團隊精神，統籌疫情防控措施，
Xi Jinping Thought on Socialism with Chinese Characteristics for       亦堅持以市場為本、以創新驅動，積極開拓市
a New Era (“中國特色社會主義思想”). The company thoroughly                       場，努力提高服務質素。
implements the spirit of our management team, coordinates
epidemic prevention and control, adheres to market-oriented,
innovation-driven, actively develops markets, and strives to improve
service quality.

Despite the volatile market environment, we were exhilarated by        儘管市場環境動盪，但本公司於2020年成功在
the successful listing on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange         聯交所主板上市令我們倍感振奮，上市亦為集
in 2020 and it has cemented a solid foundation for our Group’s         團發展奠定堅實基礎。我們亦在探索進軍建材
development. We were also exploring in trading of construction         （尤其是砂石料）貿易業務的機會。我們相信，
materials, particularly gravel. We believe this new arrangement will   此新安排亦將有助我們把握鐵路運輸服務的額
also help us secure additional demand for our railway transportation   外需求。
services.

Market Condition                                                       市場狀況
The consumption of the China markets in 2020 have been adversely       於2020年，中國市場的消耗量受到COVID-19
affected by the outbreak of the COVID-19. Since March 2020, due        疫 情 所 不 利 影 響。自2020年3月 以 來，由 於 受
to the impact of the outbreak, the enterprises in Hebei Province       到疫情影響，河北省企業嚴重開工不足，煤炭
were seriously under-operation, and coal transportation volume         運輸量因而急跌；另一方面，國家於2020年2
fell sharply; on the other hand, the country implemented a free        月至2020年5月推出免費高速公路政策，令本
high-speed policy from February 2020 to May 2020, and road             集團的鐵路運輸業務面對來自道路運輸的激烈
transportation brought fierce competition to our rail transportation   競爭。為此，我們積極開拓非煤品貨物鐵路運

Funding and Treasury Policy                                             資金及庫務政策
The Group maintain an optimal financial position and minimise its       本集團將財務狀況保持在最佳水平並將所有業
financial risks in all the business decisions. The Group regularly      務決策的財務風險降至最低。本集團定期審視
reviews the cash position to ensure there are sufficient funds to       現金狀況，以確保資金足以支持其業務營運及
support its business operations and future investments as and when      未來投資需要。
needed.

Foreign exchange risk                                                   外匯風險
The Group operated primarily in the PRC and are denominated in          本集團的業務主要於中國經營並以人民幣計
RMB. It is expected that the Group will not be subjected to any         值。本集團預期不會因匯率波動而受到重大不
material adverse effects arising from exchange rate fluctuation.        利影響。儘管如此，本集團將繼續監察外匯風
Nevertheless, the Group will continue to monitor foreign exchange       險，並將於有需要時採取適當行動。
exposure and will take appropriate action when needed.

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                   董事會多元化政策
The Company has adopted the board diversity policy (the “Board           為提高董事會的有效性，本公司已採納董事會
Diversity Policy”) which sets out the objective and approach to          多 元 化 政 策（「董 事 會 多 元 化 政 策」），當 中 載
achieve and maintain diversity on the Board in order to enhance the      列實現及維持董事會多元化的目標及方法。董
effectiveness of the Board. The Board Diversity Policy provides that     事會多元化政策規定本公司必須致力確保董事
the Company should endeavor to ensure that the Board members             會成員所擁有並就支持執行本公司業務策略所
have the appropriate balance of skills, experience and diversity         需的各項技能、經驗及多元化觀點保持適當平
of perspectives that are required to support the execution of the        衡。根據董事會多元化政策，本公司於甄選董
Company’s business strategy. Pursuant to the Board Diversity             事候選人時會考慮多項多元化因素，包括但不
Policy, selection of candidates for Directors will be based in a range   限於專業經驗、性別、年齡、文化、獨立性、
of diversity perspectives, including but not limited to professional     教育背景、知識、技能及服務年資。委任的最
experience, gender, age, culture, independence, educational              終決定將根據所選定候選人的優點及將為董事
background, knowledge, expertise and length of service. The              會作出的貢獻而作出。董事會相信，以用人唯
ultimate decision of the appointment will be based on merit and          才的準則委任董事將最能有利於本公司繼續為
the contribution which the selected candidates will bring to the         股東及其他持份者服務。
Board. The Board believes that such merit-based appointments
will best enable the Company to serve the Shareholders and other
stakeholders going forward.

The Board will continue to take steps to promote gender diversity        董事會將繼續採取措施以促進本公司各層級
at all levels of the Company, including but not limited to the Board     （包 括 但 不 限 於 董 事 會 及 高 級 管 理 層）達 致 性
and the senior management levels. While the gender diversity             別多元化。儘管由於大多數董事均為男性而令
at the Board level can be improved given the majority of the             董事會在性別多元化方面有待改善，但整體而
Directors are male, the Board will continue to apply the principle of    言，董 事 會 仍 將 繼 續 採 取 用 人 唯 才 的 委 任 原
appointments based on merits with reference to the Board Diversity       則，同時參考董事會多元化政策。董事會亦將
Policy as a whole. The Board would also ensure that appropriate          確保根據投資者的期望以及國際及地方建議最
balance of gender diversity is achieved with reference to investors’     佳常規實現性別多元化的適當平衡。
expectation, and international and local recommended best
practices.

reasonable assurance, but not absolute assurance, against material

are reviewed. The Company also engaged an independent internal             內部控制顧問（「內部控制顧問」）檢討內部控制

RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE DIRECTORS                                        董事就綜合財務報表須承擔的
FOR THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                                           責任
STATEMENTS
The directors are responsible for the preparation of the consolidated    董事須負責根據國際會計準則理事會頒佈的國
financial statements that give a true and fair view in accordance        際財務報告準則及香港公司條例的披露規定擬
with IFRSs issued by the IASB and the disclosure requirements of         備真實而中肯的綜合財務報表，並對其認為為
the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance and for such internal control          使綜合財務報表的擬備不存在由於欺詐或錯誤
as the directors determine is necessary to enable the preparation        而導致的重大錯誤陳述所需的內部控制負責。
of consolidated financial statements that are free from material
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.

In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors        在擬備綜合財務報表時，董事負責評估 貴集
are responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a       團持續經營的能力，並在適用情況下披露與持
going concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going       續經營有關的事項，以及使用持續經營為會計
concern and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the       基礎，除非董事有意將 貴集團清盤或停止經
directors either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations,   營，或別無其他實際的替代方案。
or have no realistic alternative but to do so.

STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether         我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from          在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue      得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報
an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. This report is made      告。本報告是僅向整體股東出具，除此以外，
solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not           不 可 用 作 其 他 用 途。我 們 概 不 就 本 報 告 的 內
assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person   容，對任何其他人士負責或承擔責任。
for the contents of this report.

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance but is not a          合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照香
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will         港審計準則進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述
always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements     存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤
can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,           引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能
individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected     影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴財務報表所作出
to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of      的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
these consolidated financial statements.

throughout the audit. We also:                                          亦：

     company with limited liability under the Companies Law,              （1961年第3號法例，經綜合及修訂）在開
     Cap. 22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated and revised) of the          曼群島註冊成立的獲豁免有限公司。本公
     Cayman Islands. The shares of the Company were listed on             司 股 份 於2020年10月23日 在 香 港 聯 合 交
     the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited            易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。本公
     (the “Stock Exchange”) on 23 October 2020. The Company               司及其附屬公司的主要業務為透過在中華
     and its subsidiaries are principally engaged in the provision        人民共和國（「中國」）經營貨運鐵路及鐵路

2 SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                     2 主要會計政策（續）
  (continued)
  (d) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                       (d) 附屬公司及非控股權益

      Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The             附屬公司指由本集團控制的實體。倘
      Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or has rights,        本集團通過對實體的權力就參與實體
      to variable returns from its involvement with the entity and       業務所得可變回報承受風險或享有權
      has the ability to affect those returns through its power          利，並有能力影響該等回報，則本集
      over the entity. When assessing whether the Group has              團控制該實體。於評估本集團是否有
      power, only substantive rights (held by the Group and              權力時，僅考慮實質性權利（由本集
      other parties) are considered.                                     團及其他各方所持有者）。

      An investment in a subsidiary is consolidated into the             於附屬公司的投資自獲得控制權當日
      consolidated financial statements from the date that               起於綜合財務報表綜合入賬，直至失
      control commences until the date that control ceases.              去控制權之日為止。集團內公司間的
      Intra-group balances, transactions and cash flows and any          結餘、交易及現金流量以及自集團內
      unrealized profits arising from intra-group transactions are       公司間交易產生的任何未變現溢利，
      eliminated in full in preparing the consolidated financial         均於編製綜合財務報表時全數對銷。
      statements. Unrealized losses resulting from intra-group           自集團內公司間交易產生的未變現虧
      transactions are eliminated in the same way as unrealized          損僅在並無證據顯示出現減值的情況
      gains but only to the extent that there is no evidence of          下，方會按與未變現收益相同的方式
      impairment.                                                        予以對銷。

      Non-controlling interests represent the equity in a                非控股權益指於附屬公司中並非直接
      subsidiary not attributable directly or indirectly to the          或間接歸屬於本公司的權益，而本集
      Company, and in respect of which the Group has not                 團並無就此與該等權益的持有人協定
      agreed any additional terms with the holders of those              任何額外條款，致使本集團整體就符
      interests which would result in the Group as a whole               合金融負債定義的該等權益承擔合約
      having a contractual obligation in respect of those                責任。就各業務合併而言，本集團可
      interests that meets the definition of a financial liability.      選擇按公平值或按非控股權益於附屬
      For each business combination, the Group can elect to              公司可識別資產淨值應佔的比例計量
      measure any non-controlling interests either at fair value or      任何非控股權益。
      at the non-controlling interests’ proportionate share of the
      subsidiary’s net identifiable assets.

      Non-controlling interests are presented in the consolidated        非控股權益乃於綜合財務狀況表內之
      statement of financial position within equity, separately          權益項下呈列，並獨立於本公司權益
      from equity attributable to the equity shareholders of             股東應佔權益。本集團業績中的非控
      the Company. Non-controlling interests in the results of           股權益乃於綜合損益表及綜合損益及
      the Group are presented on the face of the consolidated            其他全面收益表中呈列為本公司非控
      statement of profit or loss and the consolidated                   股權益與權益股東之間年度損益總額
      statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                及全面收益總額的分配。
      income as an allocation of the total profit or loss and
      total comprehensive income for the year between non-
      controlling interests and the equity shareholders of the
      Company.

      In the Company’s statement of financial position, an               於本公司財務狀況表內，於附屬公司
      investment in a subsidiary is stated at cost less impairment       的投資乃按成本減去減值虧損列賬
      losses (see Note 2(i)(ii)).                                        （見附註2(i)(ii)）。

     (e) Associate                                                         (e) 聯營公司

           An associate is an entity in which the Group has significant      聯營公司指本集團對其管理有重大影
           influence, but not control or joint control, over its             響力的實體，包括參與其財務及經營
           management, including participation in the financial and          決策，但非控制或共同控制其管理。
           operating policy decisions.

           An investment in an associate is accounted for in the             於聯營公司的投資乃按權益法於綜合
           consolidated financial statements under the equity                財務報表入賬。根據權益法，投資初
           method. Under the equity method, the investment is                步按成本入賬，並就本集團應佔被投
           initially recorded at cost, adjusted for any excess of the        資公司可識別資產淨值於收購日期
           Group’s share of the acquisition-date fair values of the          的公平值超出投資成本的任何差額
           investee’s identifiable net assets over the cost of the           （如有）作出調整。投資成本包括購買
           investment (if any). The cost of the investment includes          價、直接由於收購投資導致的其他成
           purchase price, other costs directly attributable to the          本及構成本集團股本投資一部分的於
           acquisition of the investment, and any direct investment          聯營公司的任何直接投資。其後，投
           into the associate that forms part of the Group’s equity          資就本集團應佔被投資公司資產淨值
           investment. Thereafter, the investment is adjusted for            的收購後變動及與投資相關的任何減
           the post acquisition change in the Group’s share of the           值虧損（見附註2(i)(ii)）作出調整。
           investee’s net assets and any impairment loss relating to
           the investment (see Note 2(i)(ii)).

           When the Group’s share of losses exceeds its interest in          當本集團應佔聯營公司的虧損超出
           the associate, the Group’s interest is reduced to nil and         其應佔權益，本集團的權益將會減至
           recognition of further losses is discontinued except to the       零，並會終止確認進一步虧損，除非
           extent that the Group has incurred legal or constructive          本集團已產生法律或推定責任，或代
           obligations or made payments on behalf of the investee.           表被投資公司償付款項則作別論。就
           For this purpose, the Group’s interest is the carrying            此而言，本集團的權益為按權益法計
           amount of the investment under the equity method                  算的投資賬面值，連同實質上構成本
           together with the Group’s long-term interests that in             集團於聯營公司的投資淨值的任何其
           substance form part of the Group’s net investment in the          他長期權益。
           associate.

           Unrealized profits and losses resulting from transactions         本集團與其聯營公司之間交易所產生
           between the Group and its associate are eliminated to the         的未變現損益，均按本集團於被投資
           extent of the Group’s interests in the investees, except          公司的權益比例對銷，惟倘未變現虧
           where unrealized losses provide evidence of an impairment         損顯示已轉讓資產出現減值，則該等
           of the asset transferred, in which case they are recognized       未變現虧損會即時於損益中確認。
           immediately in profit or loss.

  (h) Leased assets                                                      (h) 租賃資產

      At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether the           本集團會在合約開始時評估有關合約
      contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains,       是否屬於或包含租賃。倘合約給予為
      a lease if the contract conveys the right to control the use         換取代價而在一段時間內控制所識別
      of an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for          資產的使用權利，則該合約屬於或包
      consideration. Control is conveyed where the customer                含租賃。當客戶有權指示所識別資產
      has both the right to direct the use of the identified asset         的用途及自該用途獲得絕大部分經濟
      and to obtain substantially all of the economic benefits             利益時，即表示控制權已轉移。
      from that use.

              credit losses (ECLs) on the financial assets                                            融資產（包括現金及現金等值項

              contract assets.                                                                       （「預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）確 認 虧 損 撥

              Financial assets measured at fair value are not subject                                按公平值計量的金融資產毋須進
              to the ECL assessment.                                                                 行預期信貸虧損評估。

              ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit                                     預期信貸虧損乃以概率加權估計

              accordance with the contract and the cash flows that                                   收取的現金流量之間的差額）的
              the Group expects to receive).                                                         現值計量。

              Significant increases in credit risk                                      信貸風險顯著增加

              In assessing whether the credit risk of a financial                       於評估金融工具的信貸風險有否
              instrument has increased significantly since initial                      自初始確認以來顯著增加時，本
              recognition, the Group compares the risk of default                       集團會比較於報告日期與於初始
              occurring on the financial instrument assessed at                         確認日期評估的金融工具違約風
              the reporting date with that assessed at the date of                      險。於重新評估時，倘借款人不
              initial recognition. In making this reassessment, the                     大可能在本集團不採取追索行動
              Group considers that a default event occurs when the                      （如 變 現 抵 押 品（倘 持 有 任 何 抵
              borrower is unlikely to pay its credit obligations to the                 押 品））的 情 況 下 向 本 集 團 悉 數
              Group in full, without recourse by the Group to actions                   支付其信貸責任，則本集團即認
              such as realizing security (if any is held). The Group                    為發生違約事件。本集團會考慮
              considers both quantitative and qualitative information                   合理可靠的定量及定性資料，包
              that is reasonable and supportable, including                             括過往經驗及毋須付出過多成本
              historical experience and forward-looking information                     或努力即可獲得的前瞻性資料。
              that is available without undue cost or effort.

              In particular, the following information is taken into                    具體而言，於評估信貸風險自初
              account when assessing whether credit risk has                            始確認以來有否顯著增加時會考
              increased significantly since initial recognition:                        慮以下資料：

              –   failure to make payments of principal or interest                     －     未能於合約到期日支付本
                  on their contractually due dates;                                           金或利息；

                  to meet its obligation to the Group.                                        責任的能力造成重大不利
                                                                                              影響。

              risk is performed on either an individual basis or a                      基準或整體基準進行。倘按整體

  (k) Contract assets and contract liabilities                          (k) 合約資產及合約負債

        A contract asset is recognized when the Group recognizes               合約資產乃於本集團在根據合約所載
        revenue (see Note 2(s)) before being unconditionally                   付款條款有權無條件獲取代價之前確
        entitled to the consideration under the payment terms                  認收益（見附註2(s)）時予以確認。合
        set out in the contract. Contract assets are assessed for              約資產乃根據附註2(i)(i)所載的政策就
        ECLs in accordance with the policy set out in Note 2(i)(i)             預期信貸虧損予以評估，並於收取代
        and are reclassified to receivables when the right to the              價的權利成為無條件時重新分類為應
        consideration has become unconditional (see Note 2(l)).                收款項（見附註2(l)）。

        A contract liability is recognized when the customer pays              合約負債乃於客戶在本集團確認相關

        related revenue. In such cases, a corresponding receivable             款項亦會獲得確認（見附註2(l)）。
        would also be recognized (see Note 2(l)).

        under the effective interest method (see Note 2(s)).                  （見附註2(s)）。

      Current tax is the expected tax payable on the taxable             即期稅項為就年內應課稅收入採用於
      income for the year, using tax rates enacted or                    報告期末已頒佈或實質已頒佈的稅率

      and any adjustment to tax payable in respect of previous           的應付稅項作出任何調整。
      years.

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities arise from deductible          遞延稅項資產及負債分別自可扣稅及
      and taxable temporary differences respectively, being              應課稅的暫時差額產生，即資產及負
      the differences between the carrying amounts of assets             債就財務申報目的而言的賬面值與其
      and liabilities for financial reporting purposes and their         稅基之間的差額。遞延稅項資產亦自
      tax bases. Deferred tax assets also arise from unused tax          未動用稅項虧損及未動用稅項抵免產
      losses and unused tax credits.                                     生。

      Apart from certain limited exceptions, all deferred tax            除若干有限例外情況外，倘可能有未
      liabilities, and all deferred tax assets to the extent that        來應課稅溢利用作抵扣可動用的資
      it is probable that future taxable profits will be available       產，則所有遞延稅項負債及遞延稅項
      against which the asset can be utilized, are recognized.           資產均予確認。可支持確認自可扣稅
      Future taxable profits that may support the recognition            暫時差額所產生遞延稅項資產的未來
      of deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary           應課稅溢利包括因撥回現有應課稅暫
      differences include those that will arise from the reversal        時差額而產生的金額；惟此等差額須
      of existing taxable temporary differences, provided those          與同一稅務機關及同一應課稅實體有
      differences relate to the same taxation authority and the          關，並預期在可扣稅暫時差額預計撥
      same taxable entity, and are expected to reverse either in         回的同一期間或遞延稅項資產所產生

      temporary difference or in periods into which a tax loss           內撥回。在釐定現有應課稅暫時差額
      arising from the deferred tax asset can be carried back or         是否支持確認自未動用稅項虧損和抵
      forward. The same criteria are adopted when determining            免產生的遞延稅項資產時，亦會採用
      whether existing taxable temporary differences support the         同一準則，即該等差額若與同一稅務
      recognition of deferred tax assets arising from unused tax         機關及同一應課稅實體有關，並預期
      losses and credits, that is, those differences are taken into      在可動用稅項虧損或抵免的期間內撥
      account if they relate to the same taxation authority and          回，則會計及有關差額。
      the same taxable entity, and are expected to reverse in a

      utilized.

      assets and liabilities are those temporary differences             情況為不影響會計或應課稅溢利的資

        Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it                    本集團將於其於日常業務過程中提供

        of the Group’s business.

        to the Group, revenue recognized under that contract                    務報告準則第15號所述的實際權宜方

        liability under the effective interest method. The Group                況下不會就重大融資部分的任何影響
        takes advantage of the practical expedient in IFRS 15                   調整代價。
        and does not adjust the consideration for any effects of a

           (ii)   Interest income                                                 (ii)   利息收入

                  Interest income is recognized as it accrues under the                  利息收入根據實際利率法利用於
                  effective interest method using the rate that exactly                  金融資產預計年期將估計未來現
                  discounts estimated future cash receipts through                       金收入準確貼現至金融資產總賬
                  the expected life of the financial assets to the gross                 面值的利率累計確認。就並無出
                  carrying amount of the financial asset. For financial                  現信貸減值並按攤銷成本計量的

                 segment result is “adjusted operating profit”. To                  為得出經調整經營溢利，本集團

                 operating profit is adjusted for items not specifically            別分部的項目作出調整，例如一

                 administration expenses and other income. No inter-                截 至2020年 及2019年12月31日

                 31 December 2020 and 2019. Assistance provided by                  個分部向另一個分部提供的協助

             Islands.

      (iii) Statutory reserve                                              (iii) 法定儲備

             In accordance with the relevant PRC laws and                         根據中國相關法律及法規，本公
             regulations, the Company’s subsidiaries established                  司於中國內地成立及營運的附屬
             and operated in Mainland China are required to                       公 司 須 將 有 關 公 司 的10%純 利
             transfer 10% of their net profit to the statutory                    轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備結餘
             reserve until the reserve balance reaches 50% of                     達 到 各 自 註 冊 資 本 的50%。向
             the respective registered capital. The transfer to                   該儲備作出的轉撥須於向權益持
             this reserve must be made before distributions to                    有人作出分派前作出。有關儲備
             equity holders. This reserve can be utilized in setting              可用於抵銷累計虧損或附屬公司
             off accumulated losses or increase capital of the                    增資，且除清盤外，不可用於分
             subsidiaries and is non-distributable other than in                  派。
             liquidation.

      (iv) Safety production fund reserve                                  (iv) 安全生產基金儲備

             The Group is engaged in freight transportation                       本集團從事貨運業務。根據中國
             business. In accordance with the regulations issued                  財政部及國家安全生產監督管
             by Ministry of Finance and State Administration of                   理總局頒佈的規定，本集團須設
             Work Safety of the PRC, the Group is required to                     立專項儲備（即安全生產基金儲
             establish a special reserve namely safety production                 備），有關儲備乃按上一個年度
             fund reserve, which is calculated at 1% of the regular               一般貨運收益的1%及危險品貨
             freight transportation revenue and 1.5% of the                       運收益的1.5%計算。
             dangerous goods freight transportation revenue of the
             previous year.

             The safety production fund reserve is mainly used for                安全生產基金儲備主要用於購置
             the acquisitions and maintenance of safety equipment                 及維護安全設備及設施。就財務
             and facilities. For the purpose of financial statements,             報表而言，有關儲備乃按上述方
             such reserve is established through an appropriation                 法透過自保留盈利撥款而設立，
             from retained earnings based on the aforementioned                   並在產生相關開支時動用。
             method and is utilized when the related expenditures

     (e) Capital management                                                  (e) 資本管理

           The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital                本集團管理資本的主要目標為保障本
           are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a               集團持續經營業務的能力，使其可透
           going concern, so that it can continue to provide returns           過使產品及服務的定價與風險水平一
           for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,               致及按合理成本取得融資，以繼續為
           by pricing products and services commensurately with                股東帶來回報並惠及其他持份者。
           the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
           reasonable cost.

           The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages                本集團積極及定期檢討及管理其資本
           its capital structure to maintain a balance between the             架構，以在較高股東回報（可能伴隨
           higher shareholders returns that might be possible with             較高借貸水平）與穩健的資本狀況所
           higher levels of borrowings and the advantages and                  帶來的優勢及保障之間取得平衡，並
           security afforded by a sound capital position, and makes            依照經濟狀況的變化對資本架構作出
           adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in         調整。
           economic conditions.

           Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are subject         本公司或其任何附屬公司概無受任何
           to any externally imposed capital requirements.                     外部施加的資本規定規限。

25 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                            25 金融風險管理及金融工具
   AND FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL                                            公平值
   INSTRUMENTS
  Exposure to credit, liquidity and interest rate risks arises in the    本集團在日常業務過程中面對信貸、流動
  normal course of the Group’s business.                                 資金及利率風險。

  The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk             本集團所面對的該等風險及本集團用以管
  management policies and practices used by the Group to                 理該等風險的金融風險管理政策和慣例載
  manage these risks are described below.                                述如下。

  (a) Credit risk                                                        (a) 信貸風險

      Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default      信貸風險指對手方不履行合約責任導
      on its contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to      致本集團蒙受財務損失的風險。本集
      the Group. The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable         團的信貸風險主要來自貿易應收款
      to trade receivables. The Group’s exposure to credit                 項。本集團就現金及現金等值項目和
      risk arising from cash and cash equivalents and bills                應收票據所承受的信貸風險有限，原
      receivable is limited because the counterparties are banks           因是對手方為本集團管理層給予高信
      and financial institutions with high credit ratings assigned         貸評級的銀行及金融機構，故本集團
      by the management of the Group, for which the Group                  認為其信貸風險偏低。
      considers to have low credit risk.

      The Group does not provide any guarantees which would                本集團不會提供將令本集團面對信貸
      expose the Group to credit risk.                                     風險的任何擔保。

      Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項

      The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by          本集團面對的信貸風險主要受各客戶
      the individual characteristics of each customer rather than          （而非客戶經營所在的行業或國家）的
      the industry or country in which the customers operate and           個別情況所影響，因此，重大集中信
      therefore significant concentrations of credit risk primarily        貸風險主要於本集團與個別客戶有重
      arise when the Group has significant exposure to individual          大業務往來時產生。於報告期末，貿
      customers. At the end of the reporting period, 2%                    易 應 收 款 項 總 額 中 的2%（2019年：
      (2019:11%) of the total trade receivables was due from the           11%）乃來自本集團的最大客戶，而
      Group’s largest customer and 28% (2019:19%) of the total             貿 易 應 收 款 項 總 額 中 的28%（ 2019
      trade receivables was due from the five largest customers.           年：19%）乃來自五大客戶。

      Individual credit evaluations are performed on all                   本集團會對所有要求超過一定金額信
      customers requiring credit over a certain amount. These              貸的客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評
      evaluations focus on the customer’s past history of making           估集中於客戶過往支付到期款項的記
      payments when due and current ability to pay, and take               錄及現時的付款能力，並考慮客戶及
      into account information specific to the customer as well            與客戶經營所在地的經濟環境有關的
      as pertaining to the economic environment in which the               特定資料。貿易應收款項於開票日期
      customer operates. Trade receivables are due within 30               起計30天內到期。本集團一般不向客
      days from the date of billing. Normally, the Group does not          戶收取抵押品。
      obtain collateral from customers.

      The Group measures loss allowances for trade receivables             本集團按相等於全期預期信貸虧損的
      and contract assets at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs,             金額計量貿易應收款項及合約資產虧
      which is calculated using a provision matrix. As the                 損撥備，有關金額乃使用撥備矩陣計
      Group’s historical credit loss experience does not indicate          算得出。由於本集團過往信貸虧損經
      significantly different loss patterns for different customer         驗並無顯示不同客戶分部的虧損模式
      segments, the loss allowance based on past due status                有重大差異，因此於根據逾期狀況計
      is not further distinguished between the Group’s different           提虧損撥備時並無在本集團不同客戶
      customer bases.                                                      群之間作進一步區分。

29 THE IMPACT OF COVID-19 PANDEMIC                                                             29 COVID-19疫情的影響
     The COVID-19 outbreak since early 2020 has significantly                                    自2020年初以來爆發COVID-19對全球企
     affected entities and economic activities globally and brought                              業及經濟活動均造成重大影響，對本集團
     additional uncertainty to the Group’s operating. The Group                                  的經營亦帶來額外不明朗因素。本集團一
     had been closely monitoring the impact on the developments                                  直密切關注疫情對集團業務發展造成的影
     on the Group’s businesses and had put in place contingency                                  響，亦已制定應急措施，包括評估對客戶
     measures, which includes assessing the impact on customers                                  的影響及與主要客戶聯繫，並開拓更多銷
     and liaising with key customers, and developing more sales                                  售渠道。截至本報告日期，管理層並無發
     channels. Up to the date of this report, management has not                                 現 任 何 會 對 本 集 團 於2020年12月31日 的
     identified any areas that could have a material impact on                                   財務表現或狀況產生重大影響之處。
     the financial performance or position of the Group as at 31
     December 2020.

     N e v e r t h e l e s s , a s C O V I D -19 c o n t i n u e s t o e v o l v e , i t i s     然 而，隨 著COVID-19持 續 演 變，目 前 要
     challenging at this moment to predict the full extent and                                   全面預測疫情對業務和經濟的影響程度及
     duration of its impact to the business and the economy. The                                 持續時間極具挑戰性。本集團將繼續採取
     Group will continue to take measures, including but not limited                             包括（但不限於）控制經營成本、監控現金
     to, controlling its operating costs, monitoring its cash flows,                             流、密 切 注 視COVID-19的 發 展 對 其 業 務
     paying close attention to the development of the COVID-19                                   營運的影響並持續評估疫情對本集團財務
     to its operations and continuously assessing its impact to the                              報表的影響、適當調整業務計劃等措施，
